\[ \begin{aligned} &\text{Inputs: } h=\text{on-hand inventory},\quad \mathbf{q}=(q_1,\ldots,q_L)=\text{pipeline orders}. \\ &[x]^+=\max(0,x). \\[3pt] &H_L=\sum_{i=1}^{L}\frac{1}{i}. \\[3pt] &e(\mathbf{q})=\begin{cases}\displaystyle \frac{\sum_{i=1}^{L}q_i/i}{H_L}, & L>0, \\0, & L=0\end{cases}\qquad \text{(reciprocal-weighted pipeline)}. \\[6pt] &u(\mathbf{q})=\begin{cases}\displaystyle \frac{\sum_{i=\lfloor L/2\rfloor+1}^{L}q_i}{\sum_{i=1}^{L}q_i}, & L\ge 2 \text{ and } \sum_{i=1}^{L}q_i>0, \\0, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases}\qquad \text{(share of pipeline arriving later)}. \\[6pt] &I(h,\mathbf{q})=h+e(\mathbf{q}). \\[3pt] &D_0(\mathbf{q})=852.1\left(1+0.6u(\mathbf{q})\right). \\[3pt] &A(h,\mathbf{q})=\begin{cases}0.3\left(1+1.5\dfrac{220.0-I(h,\mathbf{q})}{220.0}\right), & I(h,\mathbf{q})<220.0, \\0, & I(h,\mathbf{q})\ge 220.0\end{cases}\qquad \text{(shortage boost)}. \\[6pt] &T(h,\mathbf{q})=\left(D_0(\mathbf{q})+A(h,\mathbf{q})\right)\left[1+0.1\left(\frac{1.4}{0.8}-1\right)\right]. \\[3pt] &x(h,\mathbf{q})=0.1\left(1-\frac{u(\mathbf{q})}{2}\right)[T(h,\mathbf{q})-I(h,\mathbf{q})]^+. \\[3pt] &\boxed{Q(h,\mathbf{q})=\operatorname{round}_{\mathrm{even}}\left(\begin{cases}0, & x(h,\mathbf{q})<10.0, \\x(h,\mathbf{q}), & x(h,\mathbf{q})\ge 10.0\end{cases}\right)}}. \end{aligned} \]

  1. if L > 0:
  2. # Calculate weighted pipeline with exponential decay for urgency
  3. weighted_pipeline = 0.0
  4. total_weight = 0.0
  6. for i, q in enumerate(pipeline_orders):
  7. # Exponential decay: more urgent for orders arriving soon
  8. weight = 1.0 / (1.0 + i) # Simple reciprocal weighting
  9. weighted_pipeline += q * weight
  10. total_weight += weight
  12. effective_pipeline = weighted_pipeline / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else sum(pipeline_orders)
  14. # Calculate pipeline urgency based on distribution
  15. pipeline_urgency = 0.0
  16. if L >= 2:
  17. # Measure how concentrated pipeline is in near future
  18. near_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders[:L//2])
  19. far_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders[L//2:])
  20. total_pipeline = near_pipeline + far_pipeline
  22. if total_pipeline > 0:
  23. # Higher urgency if more pipeline is in distant future
  24. pipeline_urgency = far_pipeline / total_pipeline
  25. else:
  26. effective_pipeline = 0.0
  27. pipeline_urgency = 0.0
  29. # Calculate inventory position

  35. # NEW: Safety stock deficit adjustment
  36. safety_adjusted_level = dynamic_safety_stock
  37. if inventory_position < safety_stock:
  38. deficit_ratio = max(0, (safety_stock - inventory_position) / max(1, safety_stock))
  39. adjustment = safety_adjustment_factor * (1.0 + deficit_ratio * lost_sales_multiplier)
  40. safety_adjusted_level += adjustment
  42. # Cost-balanced order-up-to level
  43. cost_ratio = lost_sales_cost_weight / holding_cost_weight
  44. cost_balanced_level = safety_adjusted_level * (1.0 + demand_anticipation_factor * (cost_ratio - 1.0))

Compact mathematical formula

\[ \begin{aligned} &\text{Inputs: } h=\text{on-hand inventory},\quad \mathbf{q}=(q_1,\ldots,q_L)=\text{pipeline orders}. \\ &I = h+\sum_{i=1}^{L}q_i \qquad \text{(inventory position)}. \\[3pt] &\bar d =\begin{cases}\displaystyle \frac{2}{L(L+1)}\sum_{i=1}^{L}(L-i+1)q_i, & L>0, \\100, & L=0\end{cases}\qquad \text{(weighted pipeline-demand estimate)}. \\[6pt] &s=\sum_{i=1}^{L-1}\operatorname{sgn}(q_{i+1}-q_i)\qquad \text{(pipeline trend score)}. \\[3pt] &d=\begin{cases}\bar d\left(1+\dfrac{0.3}{L}s\right), & L\ge 3, \\\bar d, & L<3\end{cases}\qquad \text{(trend-adjusted demand estimate)}. \\[6pt] &n=\sum_{i=1}^{\min(2,L)}q_i\qquad \text{(near-term pipeline)}. \\[3pt] &u(n)=\begin{cases}1+\dfrac{50.0-n}{50.0}\left(0.5-1\right), & n<50.0, \\1, & n\ge 50.0\end{cases}\qquad \text{(urgency multiplier)}. \\[6pt] &B=u(n)\left[583.6+1.5(d-100)\right]. \\[3pt] &G=\max\left(B,\;0.5Ld\right). \\[3pt] &R=G\left[1+0.1\left(1-\min\left(1,\frac{I}{G}\right)\right)\right]. \\[3pt] &T=0.5R+122.3\qquad \text{(target inventory position)}. \\[6pt] &\operatorname{clip}_{[96.6,200.0]}(x)=\max\left(96.6,\min\left(200.0,x\right)\right). \\[3pt] &\boxed{Q(h,\mathbf{q})=\operatorname{round}_{\mathrm{even}}\left(\operatorname{clip}_{[96.6,200.0]}\left(0.1\max(0,T-I)+(1-0.1)96.6\right)\right)}. \end{aligned} \]

  10. # table_motifs: inventory_position;pipeline_weighting;nonlinear_pipeline_composition;order_up_to;state_dependent_target;partial_adjustment;order_clipping
  11. # extra_motifs: safety_stock_buffer;pipeline_demand_proxy
  12. def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
  13. base_stock = 510.93467142866245 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 510.93467142866245, "min": 400, "max": 700, "type": "float"}
  14. safety_stock = 45.0 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 45.0, "min": 20, "max": 80, "type": "float"}
  15. demand_estimate = 97.75006872229582 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 97.75006872229582, "min": 90, "max": 110, "type": "float"}
  16. smoothing_factor = 0.05 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.05, "min": 0.05, "max": 0.3, "type": "float"}
  17. pipeline_weight = 0.5 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.1, "max": 0.5, "type": "float"}
  19. # Calculate net inventory position
  20. net_inventory = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
  22. # Calculate expected demand during lead time
  23. expected_lead_time_demand = demand_estimate * len(pipeline_orders)
  25. # Calculate target inventory position
  26. target_position = expected_lead_time_demand + safety_stock
  28. # Calculate base order amount using (s,S) logic
  29. base_order = max(0, target_position - net_inventory)
  31. # Apply base stock ceiling
  32. max_order = max(0, base_stock - net_inventory)
  34. # Adjust for pipeline coverage (reduce order if pipeline is already high)
  35. pipeline_coverage = sum(pipeline_orders) / max(1, expected_lead_time_demand)
  36. pipeline_adjustment = 1.0 - pipeline_weight * min(1.0, pipeline_coverage)
  38. # Calculate adjusted order
  39. adjusted_order = min(base_order, max_order) * pipeline_adjustment
  41. # Smooth with demand estimate to prevent extreme orders
  42. order_amount = smoothing_factor * adjusted_order + (1 - smoothing_factor) * demand_estimate

\[ \begin{aligned} &\text{Inputs: } h=\text{on-hand inventory},\quad \mathbf{q}=(q_0,\ldots,q_{L-1}) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{third} = \left\lfloor\frac{L}{3}\right\rfloor \\ \mathrm{third} = \begin{cases}1, & \mathrm{third} = 0 \\ \mathrm{third}, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{first\_avg} = \frac{\sum_{i=0}^{\left(\mathrm{third}\right)-1} q_i}{\mathrm{third}} \\ \mathrm{second\_avg} = \begin{cases}\frac{\sum_{i=\mathrm{third}}^{\left(\left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right)\right)-1} q_i}{\mathrm{third}}, & \left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right) \le L \\ \frac{\sum_{i=\mathrm{third}}^{L-1} q_i}{\left(L - \mathrm{third}\right)}, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{third\_avg} = \begin{cases}\frac{\sum_{i=\left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right)}^{L-1} q_i}{\left(L - \left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right)\right)}, & \left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right) < L \\ 0, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{trend\_ratio} = \begin{cases}\frac{\left(\mathrm{third\_avg} -   6. second_avg = sum(pipeline_orders[third:2*third]) / third if 2*third <= L else sum(pipeline_orders[third:]) / (L - third)
  7. third_avg = sum(pipeline_orders[2*third:]) / (L - 2*third) if 2*third < L else 0
  9. # Detect pattern

  11. trend_ratio = (third_avg - first_avg) / third_avg
  12. else:
  13. trend_ratio = 0
  15. # Calculate volatility
  16. pipeline_mean = sum(pipeline_orders) / L
  17. if pipeline_mean > 0 and L > 1:
  18. variance = sum((q - pipeline_mean) ** 2 for q in pipeline_orders) / L
  19. volatility = variance ** 0.5 / pipeline_mean
  20. else:

  24. if abs(trend_ratio) > 0.3:
  25. # Strong trend detected

  27. # Increasing trend - anticipate higher demand

  30. # Decreasing trend - anticipate lower demand
  31. pattern_adjustment = pattern_sensitivity * trend_ratio * base_stock * 0.5
  32. elif volatility > 0.5:

  40. volatility = 0.0

  44. # Higher critical ratio → more aggressive ordering
  45. newsvendor_factor = critical_ratio * 2 - 1 # Maps [0.5, 0.9] to [0, 0.8]

Declarative mathematical formula rows 1-12

This policy is rendered as declarative equations. Reductions use summation notation and stateful updates use recurrences.

\[ \begin{aligned} &\text{Inputs: } h=\text{on-hand inventory},\quad \mathbf{q}=(q_0,\ldots,q_{L-1}) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{third} = \left\lfloor\frac{L}{3}\right\rfloor \\ \mathrm{third} = \begin{cases}1, & \mathrm{third} = 0 \\ \mathrm{third}, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{first\_avg} = \frac{\sum_{i=0}^{\left(\mathrm{third}\right)-1} q_i}{\mathrm{third}} \\ \mathrm{second\_avg} = \begin{cases}\frac{\sum_{i=\mathrm{third}}^{\left(\left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right)\right)-1} q_i}{\mathrm{third}}, & \left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right) \le L \\ \frac{\sum_{i=\mathrm{third}}^{L-1} q_i}{\left(L - \mathrm{third}\right)}, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{third\_avg} = \begin{cases}\frac{\sum_{i=\left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right)}^{L-1} q_i}{\left(L - \left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right)\right)}, & \left(2 \cdot \mathrm{third}\right) < L \\ 0, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{trend\_ratio} = \begin{cases}\frac{\left(\mathrm{third\_avg} - \[ \begin{aligned} &\text{Inputs: } h=\text{on-hand inventory},\quad \mathbf{q}=(q_0,\ldots,q_{L-1}) \\ &\mathrm{inventory\_position} = \left(h + \sum_{i=0}^{L-1} q_i\right) \\ &\mathrm{weighted\_demand\_estimate} = 0 \\ &\mathrm{total\_weight} = 0 \\ &\mathrm{weighted\_demand\_estimate} = \sum_{i=0}^{L-1}\left(\left(q_{i} \cdot \frac{\left(L - i\right)}{L}\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{total\_weight} = \sum_{i=0}^{L-1}\left(\frac{\left(L - i\right)}{L}\right) \\ &\mathrm{avg\_weighted\_demand} = \begin{cases}\frac{\mathrm{weighted\_demand\_estimate}}{\mathrm{total\_weight}}, & \mathrm{total\_weight} > 0 \\ 100.0, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ &\mathrm{adjusted\_base\_stock} = \left(583.6 + \left(1.5 \cdot \left(\mathrm{avg\_weighted\_demand} - 100.0\right)\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{near\_pipeline} = \sum_{i=0}^{\left(\min\left(2, L\right)\right)-1} q_i \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{urgency\_adjustment} = \left(1.0 + \left(\frac{\left(50.0 - \mathrm{near\_pipeline}\right)}{50.0} \cdot \left(0.5 - 1.0\right)\right)\right) \\ \mathrm{adjusted\_base\_stock} = \left(\mathrm{adjusted\_base\_stock} \cdot \mathrm{urgency\_adjustment}\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\mathrm{near\_pipeline} < 50.0\right) \\ &\mathrm{lead\_time\_demand} = \left(\mathrm{avg\_weighted\_demand} \cdot L\right) \\ &\mathrm{coverage\_adjustment} = \left(\mathrm{lead\_time\_demand} \cdot 0.5\right) \\ &\mathrm{final\_base\_stock} = \max\left(\mathrm{adjusted\_base\_stock}, \mathrm{coverage\_adjustment}\right) \\ &\mathrm{cost\_adjusted\_base\_stock} = \left(\mathrm{final\_base\_stock} \cdot 0.5\right) \\ &\mathrm{target\_inventory\_position} = \left(\mathrm{cost\_adjusted\_base\_stock} + 122.3\right) \\ &\mathrm{raw\_order} = \max\left(0, \left(\mathrm{target\_inventory\_position} -   22. inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)
  24. # Calculate expected demand during lead time
  25. lead_time = len(pipeline_orders)
  26. expected_lead_time_demand = demand_forecast * lead_time
  28. # Adjust safety stock based on cost ratio (p/(p+h) = 2/3 ≈ 0.667)
  29. adjusted_safety_stock = safety_stock * cost_ratio_factor
  31. # Calculate target inventory level with pipeline consideration
  32. # Give more weight to near-term pipeline arrivals
  33. pipeline_imbalance = sum(p * (1 - pipeline_weight * i) for i, p in enumerate(pipeline_orders)) / lead_time if lead_time > 0 else 0
  34. target_inventory = expected_lead_time_demand + adjusted_safety_stock - max(0, pipeline_imbalance - demand_forecast)
  36. # Use the maximum of base_stock and target_inventory as order-up-to level
  37. order_up_to = max(base_stock, target_inventory)
  39. # Calculate raw order amount
  40. raw_order = max(0, order_up_to - inventory_position)
  42. # Apply smoothing to reduce order volatility

This policy is rendered as declarative equations. Reductions use summation notation and stateful updates use recurrences.

\[ \begin{aligned} &\text{Inputs: } h=\text{on-hand inventory},\quad \mathbf{q}=(q_0,\ldots,q_{L-1}) \\ &\mathrm{total\_pipeline} = \sum_{i=0}^{L-1} q_i \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{split} = \left\lfloor\frac{L}{2}\right\rfloor \\ \mathrm{first\_half} = \sum_{i=0}^{\left(\mathrm{split}\right)-1} q_i \\ \mathrm{second\_half} = \sum_{i=\mathrm{split}}^{L-1} q_i \\ \mathrm{imbalance} = \begin{cases}\frac{\left|\left(\mathrm{first\_half} - \mathrm{second\_half}\right)\right|}{\left(\mathrm{first\_half} + \mathrm{second\_half}\right)}, & \left(\mathrm{first\_half} + \mathrm{second\_half}\right) > 0 \\ 0.0, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{shape\_adjustment} = \left(\left(0.4 \cdot \mathrm{imbalance}\right) \cdot 390.0\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L \ge 2\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{shape\_adjustment} = 0.0\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(L \ge 2\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{weighted\_pipeline} = \sum_{i=0}^{L-1}\left(\left(q_{i} \cdot \left(0.9\right)^{\left(\left(L - i\right) - 1\right)}\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{trend\_adjustment} = 0.0 \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{recent\_periods} = \min\left(3, L\right) \\ \mathrm{recent\_sum} = \sum_{i=0}^{\left(\mathrm{recent\_periods}\right)-1} q_i \\ \mathrm{older\_sum} = \begin{cases}\sum_{i=\mathrm{recent\_periods}}^{L-1} q_i, & L > \mathrm{recent\_periods} \\ 0, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{recent\_avg} = \frac{\mathrm{recent\_sum}}{\mathrm{recent\_periods}} \\ \mathrm{older\_avg} = \begin{cases}\frac{\mathrm{older\_sum}}{\left(L - \mathrm{recent\_periods}\right)}, & L > \mathrm{recent\_periods} \\ \mathrm{recent\_avg}, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{demand\_trend} = \left(\mathrm{recent\_avg} - \mathrm{older\_avg}\right) \\ \mathrm{trend\_adjustment} = \left(\left(1.0 \cdot \mathrm{demand\_trend}\right) \cdot 2.0\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\left(\mathrm{older\_sum} > 0 \land \mathrm{recent\_periods} > 0\right)\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L \ge 2\right) \\ &\mathrm{effective\_inventory} = \left(h + \mathrm{weighted\_pipeline}\right) \\ &\mathrm{ratio\_adjustment} = \left(\left(0.6 - 0.7\right) \cdot 100.0\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{mean\_pipeline} = \frac{\mathrm{total\_pipeline}}{L} \\ \mathrm{variance} = \frac{\sum_{q \in \mathbf{q}} \left(\left(q - \mathrm{mean\_pipeline}\right)\right)^{2}}{L} \\ \mathrm{pipeline\_std} = \left(\mathrm{variance}\right)^{0.5} \\ \mathrm{safety\_stock} = \left(0.3 \cdot \mathrm{pipeline\_std}\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L > 1\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{safety\_stock} = 0.0\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(L > 1\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{target\_inventory} = \left(\left(\left(\left(390.0 + \mathrm{ratio\_adjustment}\right) -   6. # Split into first half and second half
  7. split = L // 2
  8. first_half = sum(pipeline_orders[:split])

  11. # Calculate imbalance (0 = balanced, >0 = imbalanced)
  12. if first_half + second_half > 0:
  13. imbalance = abs(first_half - second_half) / (first_half + second_half)
  14. else:

  32. if L > 1:
  33. pipeline_mean = sum(pipeline_orders) / L
  34. if pipeline_mean > 0:
  35. variance = sum((q - pipeline_mean) ** 2 for q in pipeline_orders) / L
  36. volatility = (variance ** 0.5) / pipeline_mean
  37. else:
  38. volatility = 0.0

  12. def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):

  39. # Round to nearest integer
  40. order_amount = int(round(order_amount))
  42. return order_amount

  12. def compute_order_amount(on_hand_inventory, pipeline_orders):
  13. base_stock = 294.00072750770767 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 294.00072750770767, "min": 10, "max": 1000, "type": "float"}
  14. safety_stock = 20.0 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 20.0, "min": 0, "max": 100, "type": "float"}

  18. inventory_position = on_hand_inventory + sum(pipeline_orders)

  25. dynamic_target = base_stock + safety_stock + pipeline_adjustment
  27. # Calculate order amount with smoothing
  28. raw_order = max(0, dynamic_target - inventory_position)
  30. # Apply demand forecast adjustment
  31. recent_demand_estimate = sum(pipeline_orders) / max(1, len(pipeline_orders)) * demand_forecast_factor
  32. adjusted_order = max(0, raw_order * 0.7 + recent_demand_estimate * 0.3) # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.7, "min": 0.1, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
  34. # Round to nearest integer (as required by output type)
  35. order_amount = int(round(adjusted_order))

  37. order_amount = order_amount * adjustment_factor
  39. # Round to nearest integer (practical ordering)
  40. return order_amount

  16. smoothing_factor = 0.05 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.05, "min": 0.05, "max": 0.3, "type": "float"}
  17. pipeline_weight = 0.5 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 0.5, "min": 0.5, "max": 1.0, "type": "float"}
  18. lost_sales_weight = 1.1 # OPT_PARAM: {"initial": 1.1, "min": 1.0, "max": 1.5, "type": "float"}
  20. # Calculate effective pipeline (weighted average)
  21. weighted_pipeline = 0
  22. total_weight = 0
  23. for i, q in enumerate(pipeline_orders):
  24. weight = pipeline_weight ** (len(pipeline_orders) - i - 1)
  25. weighted_pipeline += q * weight
  26. total_weight += weight
  28. if total_weight > 0:
  29. effective_pipeline = weighted_pipeline / total_weight * len(pipeline_orders)
  30. else:
  31. effective_pipeline = sum(pipeline_orders)
  33. # Calculate net inventory position

  38. adjusted_safety = safety_stock * (1.0 + 0.3 * (1.0 - min(1.0, pipeline_coverage)))

  22. non_zero_pipeline = [q for q in pipeline_orders if q > 0]
  23. if non_zero_pipeline:
  24. avg_pipeline = sum(non_zero_pipeline) / len(non_zero_pipeline)
  25. else:

  28. # Forecast demand based on pipeline average

  38. # Calculate order with smoothing

  40. smoothed_order = smoothing_factor * order_needed + (1 - smoothing_factor) * avg_pipeline

\[ \begin{aligned} &\text{Inputs: } h=\text{on-hand inventory},\quad \mathbf{q}=(q_0,\ldots,q_{L-1}) \\ &\mathrm{inventory\_position} = \left(h + \sum_{i=0}^{L-1} q_i\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{recent\_arrivals} = q_{:3} \\ \mathrm{weights} = \left[\left(0.6\right)^{i} \mid i \in \operatorname{range}\left(\operatorname{len}\left(\mathrm{recent\_arrivals}\right)\right)\right] \\ \mathrm{weights} = \left[\frac{w}{\operatorname{sum}\left(\mathrm{weights}\right)} \mid w \in \mathrm{weights}\right] \\ \mathrm{smoothed\_demand} = \sum_{\left(w, d\right) \in \operatorname{zip}\left(\mathrm{weights}, \mathrm{recent\_arrivals}\right)} \left(w \cdot d\right) \\ \mathrm{avg\_recent} = \frac{\operatorname{sum}\left(\mathrm{recent\_arrivals}\right)}{\operatorname{len}\left(\mathrm{recent\_arrivals}\right)} \\ \mathrm{squared\_diffs} = \left[\left(\left(x - \mathrm{avg\_recent}\right)\right)^{2} \mid x \in \mathrm{recent\_arrivals}\right] \\ \mathrm{variability} = \left(\frac{\operatorname{sum}\left(\mathrm{squared\_diffs}\right)}{\operatorname{len}\left(\mathrm{recent\_arrivals}\right)}\right)^{0.5} \\ \mathrm{expected\_demand} = \left(\left(\mathrm{smoothed\_demand} \cdot 0.6\right) + \left(\mathrm{variability} \cdot 0.2\right)\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L \ge 3\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{expected\_demand} = \left(100.0 \cdot 0.6\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(L \ge 3\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{pipeline\_adjustment} = \sum_{i=0}^{L-1}\left(\left(\left(q_{i} \cdot \left(1.0\right)^{i}\right) \cdot 0.4\right)\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{pipeline\_mean} = \frac{\sum_{i=0}^{L-1} q_i}{L} \\ \mathrm{pipeline\_variance} = \frac{\sum_{q \in \mathbf{q}} \left(\left(q - \mathrm{pipeline\_mean}\right)\right)^{2}}{L} \\ \mathrm{pipeline\_std} = \max\left(0, \left(\mathrm{pipeline\_variance}\right)^{0.5}\right) \\ \mathrm{variability\_adjustment} = \left(\mathrm{pipeline\_std} \cdot 3.6e-03\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L \ge 2\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{variability\_adjustment} = 0.0\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(L \ge 2\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{dynamic\_safety} = \left(50.8 \cdot 1.2\right) \\ &\mathrm{on\_hand\_adjustment} = \left(h \cdot 0.0\right) \\ &\mathrm{order\_up\_to} = \left(\left(\left(\left(\left(595.3 + \mathrm{dynamic\_safety}\right) + \mathrm{expected\_demand}\right) - Policy page 2/2

Python source lines 37-79

  1. avg_recent = sum(recent_arrivals) / len(recent_arrivals)
  2. squared_diffs = [(x - avg_recent) ** 2 for x in recent_arrivals]
  3. variability = (sum(squared_diffs) / len(recent_arrivals)) ** 0.5
  5. # Combine smoothed demand with variability adjustment
  6. expected_demand = smoothed_demand * demand_forecast_factor + variability * demand_variability_factor
  7. else:
  8. expected_demand = 100.0 * demand_forecast_factor
  10. # Calculate pipeline-weighted adjustment with exponential decay
  11. pipeline_adjustment = 0
  12. for i, order in enumerate(pipeline_orders):
  13. decay = pipeline_decay_factor ** i
  14. pipeline_adjustment += order * decay * pipeline_weight_factor
  16. # Calculate pipeline variability adjustment using all pipeline orders
  17. if len(pipeline_orders) >= 2:
  18. pipeline_mean = sum(pipeline_orders) / len(pipeline_orders)
  19. pipeline_variance = sum((q - pipeline_mean) ** 2 for q in pipeline_orders) / len(pipeline_orders)
  20. pipeline_std = max(0, pipeline_variance ** 0.5)
  21. variability_adjustment = pipeline_std * pipeline_std_factor
  22. else:
  23. variability_adjustment = 0.0
  25. # Dynamic safety stock adjustment
  26. dynamic_safety = safety_stock * safety_stock_multiplier
  28. # On-hand inventory adjustment (reduces order when on-hand is high)
  29. on_hand_adjustment = on_hand_inventory * on_hand_sensitivity
  31. # Calculate order-up-to level with all adjustments
  32. order_up_to = base_stock + dynamic_safety + expected_demand - pipeline_adjustment + variability_adjustment - on_hand_adjustment
  34. # Base order amount
  35. raw_order = max(0, order_up_to - inventory_position)
  37. # Apply floor and ceiling constraints
  38. constrained_order = max(order_floor, min(order_ceiling, raw_order))
  40. # Round to nearest integer
  41. order_amount = int(round(constrained_order))
  43. return order_amount

\[ \begin{aligned} &\text{Inputs: } h=\text{on-hand inventory},\quad \mathbf{q}=(q_0,\ldots,q_{L-1}) \\ &\mathrm{d\_hat} = 100.0 \\ &\mathrm{available\_now} = \left(h + q_{0}\right) \\ &\mathrm{inventory\_position} = \left(h + \sum_{i=0}^{L-1} q_i\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{near\_term} = \sum_{i=0}^{\left(\min\left(3, L\right)\right)-1} q_i \\ \mathrm{far\_term} = \begin{cases}\sum_{i=3}^{L-1} q_i, & L > 3 \\ 0, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{near\_term\_norm} = \begin{cases}\frac{\mathrm{near\_term}}{\operatorname{float}\left(3\right)}, & L \ge 3 \\ \frac{\mathrm{near\_term}}{L}, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{far\_term\_norm} = \begin{cases}\frac{\mathrm{far\_term}}{\max\left(1, \left(L - \left(m \cdot \mathrm{d\_hat}\right)\right)\right)\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{base} = \left(89.3 + 5.7\right) \\ &\mathrm{inv\_adjust} = \left(-\left(0.0\right) \cdot h\right) \\ &\mathrm{pipe\_adjust} = \left(-\left(0.0\right) \cdot \sum_{i=0}^{L-1} q_i\right) \\ &\mathrm{shortfall\_adjust} = \frac{\left(0.0 \cdot \mathrm{projected\_shortfall}\right)}{\operatorname{float}\left(3\right)} \\ &\mathrm{shape\_adjust} = \left(-\left(0.0\right) \cdot \mathrm{pipeline\_shape}\right) \\ &\mathrm{raw\_order} = \left(\left(\left(\left(\mathrm{base} + \mathrm{inv\_adjust}\right) + \mathrm{pipe\_adjust}\right) + \mathrm{shortfall\_adjust}\right) + \mathrm{shape\_adjust}\right) \\ &\mathrm{order\_amount} = \max\left(0.0, \min\left(\mathrm{raw\_order}, \left(2.0 \cdot 89.3\right)\right)\right) \\ &\boxed{Q\left(h, \mathbf{q}\right) = \mathrm{order\_amount}} \end{aligned} \]

  31. # Adjust base stock using newsvendor logic scaled by factor
  32. newsvendor_adjusted_base = base_stock * (1.0 + newsvendor_factor * (critical_ratio - 0.5))

  35. if L == 0:

0.5\right)\right)\right)\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\boxed{Q\left(h, \mathbf{q}\right) = \operatorname{round}_{\mathrm{even}}\left(\left(\mathrm{newsvendor\_adjusted\_base} - h\right)\right)}\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L = 0\right) \\ &\mathrm{total\_pipeline} = \sum_{i=0}^{L-1} q_i \\ &\mathrm{coverage\_deficit} = 0.0 \\ &\mathrm{cumulative\_inventory} = h \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{available} = \begin{cases}\left(\mathrm{cumulative\_inventory} + q_{0}\right), & k = 0 \\ \left(\mathrm{cumulative\_inventory} + q_{k}\right), & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{expected\_coverage\_needed} = \frac{\left(\mathrm{newsvendor\_adjusted\_base} \cdot \left(k + 1\right)\right)}{L} \\ \left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{urgency\_weight} = \frac{1.0}{\left(k + 1\right)} \\ \mathrm{deficit} = \left(\left(\mathrm{expected\_coverage\_needed} -   2. # NOVEL FEATURE 1: Pipeline volatility measurement
  3. # Measure how much the pipeline fluctuates (standard deviation relative to mean)
  4. if L >= 2 and total_pipeline > 0:
  5. pipeline_mean = total_pipeline / L
  6. if pipeline_mean > 0.001:
  7. variance = sum((q - pipeline_mean) ** 2 for q in pipeline_orders) / L
  8. std_dev = variance ** 0.5
  9. volatility = std_dev / pipeline_mean
  10. # High volatility suggests unstable ordering - smooth more aggressively

  12. else:
  13. volatility_adjustment = 1.0
  14. else:
  15. volatility_adjustment = 1.0

  18. # Detect recurring patterns in pipeline distribution (e.g., increasing, decreasing, U-shaped)
  19. if L >= 3:
  20. # Calculate pattern scores for different archetypes
  21. patterns = {}
  23. # Increasing pattern (orders getting larger over time)
  24. inc_score = 0
  25. for i in range(1, L):
  26. if pipeline_orders[i-1] > 0.001:
  27. inc_score += (pipeline_orders[i] - pipeline_orders[i-1]) / pipeline_orders[i-1]
  28. inc_score /= max(1, L-1)
  29. patterns['increasing'] = max(0, inc_score) # Only positive changes
  31. # Decreasing pattern (orders getting smaller over time)
  32. dec_score = 0
  33. for i in range(1, L):
  34. if pipeline_orders[i-1] > 0.001:
  35. dec_score += (pipeline_orders[i-1] - pipeline_orders[i]) / pipeline_orders[i-1]
  36. dec_score /= max(1, L-1)
  37. patterns['decreasing'] = max(0, dec_score)
  39. # U-shaped pattern (small in middle, large at ends)
  40. u_score = 0

  42. mid = L // 2
  43. early_avg = sum(pipeline_orders[:mid]) / mid
  44. middle_avg = sum(pipeline_orders[mid:L-mid]) / max(1, L-2*mid)
  45. late_avg = sum(pipeline_orders[L-mid:]) / mid

  13. pattern_adjustment = 1.0 - pattern_weight * strongest_pattern[1] * pattern_decay
  14. elif strongest_pattern[0] == 'decreasing':

  22. else:

h\right)\right)}\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L = 0\right) \\ &\mathrm{total\_pipeline} = \sum_{i=0}^{L-1} q_i \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{pipeline\_mean} = \frac{\mathrm{total\_pipeline}}{L} \\ \left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{variance} = \frac{\sum_{q \in \mathbf{q}} \left(\left(q - \mathrm{pipeline\_mean}\right)\right)^{2}}{L} \\ \mathrm{std\_dev} = \left(\mathrm{variance}\right)^{0.5} \\ \mathrm{volatility} = \frac{\mathrm{std\_dev}}{\mathrm{pipeline\_mean}} \\ \mathrm{volatility\_adjustment} = \left(1.0 - \left(0.0 \cdot \min\left(\mathrm{volatility}, 2.0\right)\right)\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\mathrm{pipeline\_mean} > 1.0e-03\right) \\ \left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{volatility\_adjustment} = 1.0\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(\mathrm{pipeline\_mean} > 1.0e-03\right)\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\left(L \ge 2 \land \mathrm{total\_pipeline} > 0\right)\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{volatility\_adjustment} = 1.0\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(\left(L \ge 2 \land \mathrm{total\_pipeline} > 0\right)\right)\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{patterns} = \left\{\right\} \\ \mathrm{inc\_score} = 0 \\ \mathrm{inc\_score} = \sum_{i=1}^{L-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\{q_{\left(i - 1\right)} > 1.0e-03\}}\left(\frac{\left(q_{i} - q_{\left(i - 1\right)}\right)}{q_{\left(i - 1\right)}}\right)\right) \\ \mathrm{inc\_score} = \frac{\mathrm{inc\_score}}{\max\left(1, \left(L - 1\right)\right)} \\ \mathrm{patterns}_{\text{increasing}} = \max\left(0, \mathrm{inc\_score}\right) \\ \mathrm{dec\_score} = 0 \\ \mathrm{dec\_score} = \sum_{i=1}^{L-1}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\{q_{\left(i - 1\right)} > 1.0e-03\}}\left(\frac{\left(q_{\left(i - 1\right)} - q_{i}\right)}{q_{\left(i - 1\right)}}\right)\right) \\ \mathrm{dec\_score} = \frac{\mathrm{dec\_score}}{\max\left(1, \left(L - 1\right)\right)} \\ \mathrm{patterns}_{\text{decreasing}} = \max\left(0, \mathrm{dec\_score}\right) \\ \mathrm{u\_score} = 0 \\ \left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{mid} = \left\lfloor\frac{L}{2}\right\rfloor \\ \mathrm{early\_avg} = \frac{\sum_{i=0}^{\left(\mathrm{mid}\right)-1} q_i}{\mathrm{mid}} \\ \mathrm{middle\_avg} = \frac{\sum_{i=\mathrm{mid}}^{\left(\left(L - \mathrm{mid}\right)\right)-1} q_i}{\max\left(1, \left(L - \left(2 \cdot \mathrm{mid}\right)\right)\right)} \\ \mathrm{late\_avg} = \frac{\sum_{i=\left(L - \mathrm{mid}\right)}^{L-1} q_i}{\mathrm{mid}} \\ \mathrm{u\_score} = \begin{cases}\left(\frac{\left(\mathrm{early\_avg} + \mathrm{late\_avg}\right)}{\left(2 \cdot \mathrm{middle\_avg}\right)} - 1.0\right), & \mathrm{middle\_avg} > 1.0e-03 \\ \mathrm{u\_score}, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases} \\ \mathrm{u\_score} = \max\left(0, \mathrm{u\_score}\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L \ge 3\right) \\ \mathrm{patterns}_{\text{u\_shaped}} = \mathrm{u\_score} \\ \mathrm{strongest\_pattern} = \max\left(\operatorname{patterns.items}\left(\right), \mathrm{key}=\left(x \mapsto x_{1}\right)\right) \\ \left.\begin{gathered}\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{pattern\_adjustment} = \left(1.0 - \left(\left(0.4 \cdot \mathrm{strongest\_pattern}_{1}\right) \cdot 0.7\right)\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\mathrm{strongest\_pattern}_{0} = \text{increasing}\right) \\ \left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{pattern\_adjustment} = \begin{cases}\left(1.0 + \left(\left(0.4 \cdot \mathrm{strongest\_pattern}_{1}\right) \cdot 0.7\right)\right), & \mathrm{strongest\_pattern}_{0} = \text{decreasing} \\ 1.0, & \text{otherwise}\end{cases}\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(\mathrm{strongest\_pattern}_{0} = \text{increasing}\right)\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\mathrm{strongest\_pattern}_{1} > 0.2\right) \\ \left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{pattern\_adjustment} = 1.0\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(\mathrm{strongest\_pattern}_{1} > 0.2\right)\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L \ge 3\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{pattern\_adjustment} = 1.0\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(L \ge 3\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{critical\_ratio} = \frac{2.0}{\left(1.0 + 2.0\right)} \\ &\mathrm{cost\_adjusted\_base} = \left(549.5 \cdot \left(1.0 + \left(0.5 \cdot \left(\mathrm{critical\_ratio} - 0.5\right)\right)\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{adjusted\_target} = \left(\left(\mathrm{cost\_adjusted\_base} \cdot \mathrm{volatility\_adjustment}\right) \cdot \mathrm{pattern\_adjustment}\right) \\ &\mathrm{current\_position} = \left(h + \mathrm{total\_pipeline}\right) \\ &\mathrm{raw\_order} = \max\left(0, \left(\mathrm{adjusted\_target} - \mathrm{current\_position}\right)\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{pipeline\_avg} = \frac{\mathrm{total\_pipeline}}{L} \\ \mathrm{smoothed\_order} = \left(\left(0.1 \cdot \mathrm{raw\_order}\right) + \left(\left(1 - 0.1\right) \cdot \mathrm{pipeline\_avg}\right)\right)\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(L > 0\right) \\ &\left.\begin{gathered}\mathrm{smoothed\_order} = \mathrm{raw\_order}\end{gathered}\right\}\quad \left(\neg\left(L > 0\right)\right) \\ &\mathrm{order\_amount} = \operatorname{round}_{\mathrm{even}}\left(\max\left(0, \mathrm{smoothed\_order}\right)\right) \\ &\boxed{Q\left(h, \mathbf{q}\right) = \mathrm{order\_amount}} \end{aligned} \]
